# RAZStudio device-name database — Android model code -> marketing name,
# for the watermark EXIF panel (EXIF Model is often a cryptic code, e.g.
# 'Infinix X6873' -> 'GT 30 Pro'). Source: Google Play Console official
# supported_devices.csv (Retail Branding, Marketing Name, Device, Model).
# Rows where marketing name already equals the code are omitted. Not year-
# filtered (the source has no date); extra/older entries are harmless — a
# phone's EXIF only matches its own code. Format: brand<TAB>model<TAB>marketing.
# '#' lines are comments / brand section headers.
